**[01:32:14.060] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Ang aming testigo po ay certified public accountant or CPA at abogado. Nasanay-nasanay sa pag-conduct ng financial audits, reviews at investigations. Inaalok po namin ang kanyang testimonya bilang expert at para patunayan ang mga sumusunod. Una po, na-qualified siya to follow the money and reconcile the financial records. The witness possesses the education, professional qualifications, specialized training, experience and technical expertise necessary to examine financial records, trace transactions, reconcile income and assets, analyze financial relationships, and identify material discrepancies among financial documents. Sa madaling salita, your honors, expertise po niya ang sundan ang pera. Number two, he examined the respondent statements of assets, liabilities, and net worth or SALNs, income tax returns, and other tax records, bank records, anti-money laundering reports, corporate documents, audited financial statements, and other subpoenaed official or publicly available records. Three, he used recognized financial and accounting methods to test whether these records reconcile. The witness applies recognized methods of financial analysis, reconciliation, transaction tracing, and accounting analysis to compare the respondents' documented income and sources of funds against their assets, financial transactions, investments, and business interests. Fourth, based on the foregoing, the witness arrived at the following conclusions. One, Vice President Sara Duterte-SALN for the years 2022. 2023, 2024, and 2025 were untruthful with a substantial amount of assets belonging to the respondent and spouse found to be unreported. Second, Vice President Sara Duterte, during her vice presidency, continued to participate in various businesses. Third, her unexplained wealth, based only on the limited documents examined, excluding documents in the possession of Vice President Sara and spouse, amount to 818.87 million pesos. Fourth, the above-mentioned infringements which happened in 2022, 2023, 2024, and 2025 established a pattern of constitutional violations year on year. And fifth, apart from the Constitution, other laws were violated or may have been violated such as the National Internal Revenue Code, Republic Act 1379, Republic Act 6713, Republic Act 3019, Republic Act 12009, Securities and Regulations Code Rules, and Commission Audit Rules. We will establish, Your Honor, ng aming testigo ay mapagkakatiwalaan dahil siya ay may integridad. Hindi po siya yung tipong expert na pinulot lang kung saan saan. His credentials and trustworthiness are, in our view, impeccable. Ito po ang magiging ambag ng expert witness na ito. The other witnesses in this article presented the underlying records. SALN, corporate records from the SEC, records for firearms from the Philippine National Police, records of the Anti-Money Laundering Council, banks and insurance companies, and the Bureau of Internal Revenue. Atty. Cabrera will connect the dots and provide this court. with the big picture. That is all for the offer, Your Honor.

Balikan po natin yung inyong trabaho sa Isla Lipana. You said you entered that firm as a staff auditor. Generally po, anong ginagawa ng isang auditor sa firm na ganyan?

**[01:49:25.170] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Ang auditor po ay nagkakandak po ng audit, external audit. Ito po yung tinatawag na regular audit.

**[01:49:34.250] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Ang ginagawa niya pong audit ay hindi po para sa kumpanya kundi po para po sa public. Para sabihin po sa public na ang financial statements ng kumpanyang ito ay mapagkakatiwalaan.

**[01:49:45.980] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
At for what purpose po tinitingnan ng isang auditor ang mga financial statements ng isang kumpanya?

**[01:49:52.760] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Opo, required po kasi itong financial statements na mag-support po sa income tax return at sinasubmit din po ito sa Securities and Exchange Commission.

**[01:50:09.410] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Thank

**[01:50:10.110] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
you, sir.

**[01:50:11.250] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
What

**[01:50:11.950] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
kinds of opinions, if any, do auditors give?

**[01:50:17.040] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Well, yung una po, yung clean opinion po. Ang ibig sabihin po ng clean opinion is yung financial statements are fairly stated in all general respects.

**[01:50:30.140] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Yung isa po ay qualified opinion.

**[01:50:34.210] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Which means may problema po yung financial statements. Either meron pong hindi nakita yung auditor at yung hindi niya po nakita ay material. Or nakita niya po at may nakita po siyang discrepancy at yung nakita niya pong discrepancy ay material. Pero sa dalawa pong sitwasyon na to, yung kanya pong nakitang discrepancy o hindi niya nakitang document, material man, ay hindi po pervasive.

**[01:51:23.300] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Thank

**[01:51:24.180] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
you. Sa madaling salita, nag-iimbestiga kayo ng mga financial statements at documents Para malaman kung may tinatago ang kumpanya o individual, tama po ba yun?

**[01:51:36.530] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Tama po yun. Tinitignan din po natin yung process po ng internal controls po ng kumpanya para malaman po kung mapagkakatiwalaan po yung controls. Kasi kung mapagkakatiwalaan po yung controls, lalo kayo magtitiwala sa numbers.

**[01:51:55.720] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Kanina po nabanggit ninyo yung regular audit. What other kinds of audits are there, if any?

**[01:52:02.750] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Meron pong... Special audits which is based on agreed upon procedures. Meron din naman pong forensic audits or fraud audits na mas masinsin po sa isang special audit. Meron din po namang internal audit po. Yung internal audit po is para po sa management.

**[01:52:24.110] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Nabanggit po ninyo yung forensic audit at sabi nyo kanina na naghahanap kayo ng fraud. Pakipaliwanag po ng konti kung ano pa nga bang forensic audit na yan?

**[01:52:36.260] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Ang forensic audit po ay ano eh, para pong nage-examine po kayo ng iba't ibang mga dokumento. Pero ang pagkakaiba po nito ay pinagtatagpi-tagpi niyo po yung mga dokumentong iyon para malaman po ang katotohanan base po sa kwento na inyong matutunghayan. So ito pong forensic audit ay kadalasan po ginagamit po ito sa courts. Ginagamit po ito privately pagka meron pong nag-i-invest po halimbawa sa isang kumpanya para malaman nung investor kung may problema yung kumpanya talaga pagka meron silang nabalitaan na may nangyayaring katiwalian dun sa kumpanya niyan. Tsaka ginagamit din po ito ng mga litigants po para po makakolekta sa mga stockholder po na may-ari po ng kumpanya. Para po ma-pierce po ba yung veil na tinatawag at maging liable hindi lang po yung corporation kung hindi po yung mga stockholders directly po.

**[01:53:40.580] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Maraming salamat po. Let's talk about your training first. What additional training if any did you undergo at Isla Lipana and Company? in conducting these regular and forensic audits and the other audit-related services that you mentioned.

**[01:53:55.740] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Opo, sa firm po kasi marami po kaming training, hindi po mabilang po. Pero bukod po sa mga trainings po inside the firm, ang pinakamahalaga po talagang training sa forensic is yung pang-araw-araw nyo pong ginagawa. Yun po talaga ang mahalagang training because only actual experience can really teach you how to do a forensic audit.

**[01:54:19.080] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**

**[01:54:27.880] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**

Tinitingnan namin kung yung supply chain nila yung nagpo-provide po ng halimbawa toll manufacturer or service manufacturers nila kung nagko-comply po sa labor code sa batas po or sila po ba ay nag-ooperate ng sweatshop or nag-hire ba ng mga minor de edad minsan po ay may nag-forensic audit po kami ng mga computers po ng

**[01:55:10.780] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
kumpanya. Naghahanap po kami ng evidence po ng nagda-download po ng child pornography. So yung forensic audit po ay hindi lagi pong financial. Pero ang kadalasan ko pong ginagawa ay financial forensic.

**[01:55:28.760] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Baka hindi niyo kayang sagutin po itong susunod na tanong ko.

**[01:55:33.020] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
More or less

**[01:55:34.020] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
po mga ilang taon kayo nagko-conduct na ng ganitong mga klaseng audit. kasama na po ang forensic audit.

**[01:55:43.220] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Actually po nung nagsimula po akong maging auditor eh fraud audit po kagadang napasok. Ang sarap nga po magkwento kung pwede lang eh. Pero sa 37 years ko po talagang ginagawa po ito kasama po ng aking mga ibang trabaho eh talagang libo-libo na po talaga.

**[01:56:04.200] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Napakahirap na po mabilang nung oras na ginugol ko po. Dito lang po sa Trabahong ito po. Napakalaking oras na nang ginugol.

**[01:56:50.780] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**

**[01:56:54.880] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Ang Harvard Business School po ay isa po sa mga top international business schools. Ang kanila pong method ay case study method. Ang ibig pong sabihin nun ay gumagamit po sila ng mga hango sa tunay na buhay na problema po ng business upang maging pamilyar po ang mga estudyante. So yung umaaten po ng Harvard Business School, this is actually a post-graduate course. So yung po mga graduate na at mga naghahanda na pong magkaroon ng mataas na katungkulan sa kumpanya, kadalasan po siya yung kumukuha po nito.

**[01:57:34.850] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**

IESE po sa Barcelona at CIBS po sa China. So tatlong lugar po yung pinuntahan po ng programang yun. Tatlong lugar po yung venue ng pag-aaral na po yun.

**[02:06:16.790] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**

Una po siguro yung M&A po. Mergers and

**[02:06:31.850] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**

**[02:06:33.230] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Pag

**[02:06:33.790] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
-usapan po natin yung M&A na yan. Sa madaling salita dahil malamang hindi alam ng mga kababayan po yung mergers and acquisitions. Ano po ito?

**[02:06:44.100] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Ang M&A po isang servisyong binibigay po natin sa isang investor or sa isa pong balak magbenta po ng kanyang kumpanya. Pero patukoy po sa isang investor, Kung isang investor po ay mag-i-invest sa isang company sa Pilipinas at may bibilhin po siyang kumpanya, kami po yung nagsusuri po sa kanila para sa kanila nung kumpanyang yun. Kasi po itong mga investor na to ay meron po silang negosyo. Doon po sila magaling. Pero hindi po nila kabisado kung paano po alamin kung ano talaga yung nangyayari doon sa kumpanyang yun. Una po sa lahat, yung financial statement bang yan ay nagsasabi ng katotohanan. Sino ang may-ari sa kumpanyang yan? Mabubuting tao ba yan? O may mga asulto rin? O may mga problema rin? Ano ang tunay na value ng kumpanyang yan? Hindi lamang based sa financial statements. Ano ang potential ng kumpanyang yan? O kung lugi man siya, may potential ba? O kung kumikita man siya ngayon, baka bukas eh lugi na. Marami pong tinitignan na aspeto katulad kasama na rin po dyan yung kanilang pagsunod sa batas sa lahat po ng uri ng batas at regulations.

**[02:08:07.950] Atty. Alexander B. Cabrera**  
Tinitignan po din natin yung kanilang skill set, yung po bang mga taong nandyan sa kumpanyang yan ay magagaling o qualified po kasi kailangan po i-consider lahat po yan sa kanila pong sustainability. Sumusunod ba yan sa environment law? So puro usok yung factory niyan, baka may ipasari yan. O meron po ba yan mga utang na hindi nakasaad sa financial statements? Meron po bang litigation yung kumpanya niya na pending? Kaya po namin tinitignan lahat ito kasi po pag binili po yan ng kumpanya, mamanahin po nila lahat ng problema pong iyon.

**[02:08:46.060] Rep. Jose Manuel “Chel” I. Diokno**  
Para po rin sa kalaban ng lahat, dahil ako rin po ay hindi marunong dito sa mga bagay na ito.

This will be counted against your five minutes. The court would like to place and spread into the records Rule 133, Section 5. Weight to be given opinion of expert witness. How determined? I'm reading it verbatim. "In any case where the opinion of an expert witness is received in evidence, the court has a wide latitude of discretion in determining the weight to be given to such opinion, and for that purpose may consider the following." This also applies of course to the forensic expert that the respondents will present. "A. Whether the opinion is based upon sufficient facts or data. B. Whether the product of reliable whether it is the product of reliable principles and methods, C, whether the witness has applied the principles and methods reliably to the facts of the case, and D, such other factors as the court may deem helpful to make such determinations." So the chair confirms the statement made by the Hon. Lacson that it is not conclusive, it is not binding, and it is up to each senator-judge to give the accorded weight they want to give to the expert's opinion, whether it be the expert of the prosecution or the expert to be presented by the respondent.
